Of those 13, 12 now PASS (C2, C71, C179, C236, C240, C404, C422, C453, C456, C457, C458, C465) and C454 stays PARTIAL for a different reason than in round 5. The verdict stays FAIL. Closed since round 5: the three surviving mutants (Wh, Wp, A3 are all killed now), the Surface route `GET /api/decks/:deckId/missing?lookups=read` (check C468 and a Coverage row), and 12 of the 13 PARTIAL checks.

(a) Inside the owner's accepted basis of 2026-10-27 (answer 3), not marked PASS: the screen-arrangement checks missing for most journeys, the Test policy row 'Screen copy and arrangement', and every Uncovered cell of the binding sources (carried, and the residues of the round 6 screens, cut down by this round). Scope and basis: header scoped by `verify.md` 'Re-verifying after a fix'. The diff is `94639402..f064a126` (8 commits: one product file, `settings.module.css`, 3 lines; 13 test files; the checks and notes).
Every `Proof:` line (596, the 574 of round 5 plus 22 added by the fix) was re-run in full at f064a126, one invocation per suite with JSON reporters, and matched to individual test results: 596 matched and passed, 0 select no test. The first full API e2e run had 1 failing test in 551 (see (2) above, no Proof line selects it); the second full run passed 551 of 551.
Re-judged with a located assertion at f064a126: 36 checks: the 13 PARTIAL of round 5 (C2, C71, C179, C236, C240, C404, C422, C453, C454, C456, C457, C458, C465), the new C468, the 21 whose Proof file the fix touched and whose claim and tested body it did not change (citations refreshed where lines moved: C1, C3, C96, C97, C100, C137, C182, C189, C202, C204, C206, C207, C218, C219, C221, C242, C244, C392, C418, C420, C455), and C201, whose tested stylesheet changed (`settings.module.css`). Faults N10 (the controller ignores `read` and queues) fails `:131`; N11 (the ownership guard removed) fails `:133`. Same-shape is asserted as equal key sets, not equal values. |

## Faults injected

Round 6: isolated detached worktree `.claude/worktrees/verify-persona-rethink` at f064a126 (dependencies and the engine build linked from the real tree), one fault at a time, the narrowest covering proof run (jest and vitest in the scratch tree; Playwright with the web server started from the scratch tree and the API from the real build), the worktree reset (`git reset --hard`, `git clean -fd` keeping the links) after each, removed at the end; the real tree's `git status --porcelain` was empty before and after. The three survivors of round 5 (Wh, Wp, A3) were re-injected: all three are now killed. New surfaces, one fault each at least: the account exception (N1), the home grouping (N2), the sign-in destination (N3), the quote rows (N5), the password-code e-mail (N6), the name field of the sheet (N8), the tried-code counting (A3b), the `?lookups=read` route (N10, N11), the replaced-by mark (N12), the credits bar (P1, P2, P2b, P2c), the turn-off step (P3, P3b, P4, P4b), the group page and friend view (PG1, FV1), the update menu and window (P5, P5b, P6) and the Resolver look (P7). One survives: P2c. In all, 27 faults were injected and 26 killed.

## Other features (findings, not verdicts on persona-rethink)

- `.specs/features/card-alternatives/checks.md` C58 has a proof again: persona-rethink C457 restores `tests/e2e/card-alternatives-flow.spec.ts` ('pick an alternative and undo it'), which passed at f064a126 (both full runs of the e2e-chromium project).
- `.specs/features/ai-chat-screens/checks.md` C21: the claim now says 'no spend figure' and allows a store price (D28), so it no longer contradicts persona-rethink C327; the new test `no-money.spec.tsx` (a priced swap card) passed.

## New findings in round 6

- C454 (new, outside the owner's basis): the claim says both stop lines are 'in the amber ink'; `r5-credits-b-phone.html` draws the first in `--warn-ink` and the second in `--fg3` (muted), and the built page follows the picture. The claim contradicts the binding screen on the second line, and the test cannot tell (`settings-credits.spec.ts:95`, fault P2c survives). The fix is a wording change in the claim (the owner's edit, since `checks.md` is frozen) plus an assertion of the muted colour.
- Intermittent test (new, outside the owner's basis): in the first full API e2e run `shared-lists.e2e-spec.ts` 'on a sell list adds the copies missing in non-retired decks...' failed once (550 passed, 1 failed); the decks came back as Rhinar CC then Kayo CC instead of Kayo CC then Rhinar CC (`:520-523`). The cause is a query with no `ORDER BY`: `visitor-decks-missing.service.ts:46-49` `decks.find` has no `order`, so the order is whatever the table returns. The second full run passed 551 of 551 and 3 solo runs of the file passed. No Proof line selects that test; under the rule that every failure seen is this session's to fix, it needs an `order: { id: 'ASC' }` or an order-free assertion.
- Does placing the new browser assertions inside existing walks leave a claimed part unasserted? No. Every part of C453 and C465 that the builder moved into `expectResolverLook` (`collection-in-layout.spec.ts:132,254-315`) and into `expectUpdateMenuLook` and `expectUpdateWindowLook` (`:228,245,329-377`) is reached by its own Proof line, because those lines select the walk by name. The credits, turn-off and group-ask runs use stubbed sessions and add no account. The cost of the placement is different: a failure earlier in a walk hides the assertions after it in that run, and one walk is the Proof of several parts. The limit is real: the full web run made 18 sign-ups answered 201 of the 20 per hour the API allows (counted in the API log), so two more sign-ups in one run, or a second full run against the same API process, would answer 429. This round started a fresh API for each batch of fault runs.
- Screen contradictions kept by delegated decisions rather than by the owner (carried, not on the owner's accepted list): C82 (D5, 'Deck before Inventário as built', kept because a test asserts it and it costs nothing, while `r2-list-view-desktop` puts Inventário first); C74 (D4, 'keep GEM out of the copy-list tab name', because whether the site reads that text could not be confirmed, the site returned 403, while the state table and both export screens draw it); C71 (D1, 'keep the grouping by stage', because the grouping predates the feature and is tested, while the picked screen draws one flat list); C288 (D17, 'Vendida' on unsold lines only, a deliberate narrowing of the document's 'always', because a button that does nothing confuses). Sources: `decisions-2026-10-27.md` rows D1, D4, D5, D17.
- Pointer gaps (the assertion exists at a located line in a sibling test that the Proof line's name pattern does not select), which keep PASS: C56, C94, C221, C231, C244, C261, C281, C288, C297, C303, C348, C351, C352, C369, C376, C392, C394, C403, C408, C419, C421, C440, C459, C462, C466 (carried), and new this round C236 ('no eyebrow', `ShareDialog.spec.tsx:158`) and C465 (the row text, `CsvSourceList.spec.tsx:101`). The one-hour clamp of Retry-After (`sign-in-code.service.spec.ts`) is the same kind of gap, kept in Coverage because the clamp is a member of a set the code defines, not a part a claim states.
- C208 (carried PASS): no test asserts the search-URL format built at `deck-missing.service.ts:98`; the row test echoes a URL it built itself.
- Stale plan text (carried): plan.md:255 says `_devCode` rides in the answer 'when NODE_ENV is not production'; the code (auth.service.ts:68, security review fix 054a026b) requires EXPOSE_DEV_SECRETS=true, and the same flag gates `_devVerificationLink`, so the Playwright proof of C398 needs the API started with it (it was).
- C382 (D20, accepted as built) still has no ' · done' mark in `checks.md`. The password-code e-mail template is now rendered by the banned-word test (C422).

## Gate

Run at f064a126 in the close-the-deck worktree with JSON reporters, one invocation per suite, `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=4096` and `EXPOSE_DEV_SECRETS=true`: engine jest 397 passed; web vitest 2817 passed (round 5: 2808); api jest unit 1415 passed (1414); api jest int 142 passed; api jest e2e (runInBand), first run 550 passed and 1 failed (`shared-lists.e2e-spec.ts`, see New findings), second run 551 passed, 0 failed, plus 3 solo runs of that file, 27 passed each; web playwright (e2e-chromium, mobile-360, mobile-390, visual-dark-desktop) 207 passed, 20 skipped by design, 0 failed, 0 flaky; web lint, web typecheck, api typecheck and api lint exit 0. No load timeout occurred.
Proof matching: 596 `Proof:` lines in `checks.md` (round 5: 574), 596 matched individual passed tests, 0 select no test; the API e2e proofs matched in both full runs.
Completion gate: `python3 ~/.claude-personal/skills/tlc-spec-lean/scripts/validate_verification.py persona-rethink --root <worktree>` exits 1 with `ERROR persona-rethink: verdict is FAIL - route the ranked gaps back as fixes, then re-verify` and `validate_verification: 1 error(s), 0 warning(s) across [persona-rethink]`: the one error is the FAIL verdict, which the rows above support.
The real tree's `git status --porcelain` was empty before the runs and after the faults; the scratch worktree's porcelain showed only the seven linked `node_modules` and `dist` entries after every fault, and the worktree was removed at the end. No background process of this run was left; the Chrome processes of another Playwright MCP session on this machine are not mine.
